Commands read CSV on\nstandard output, diagnostics on standard error, and exit codes 0 to 4. Every command runs as one\ndatabase transaction. All persistence goes through the shipped persistence library, and the\ndatabase is selected by `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- Python 3.11 or newer: implementation language. Governed by `python.md`.\n- SQLite: database engine, reached through `persistence.open_database` and the shipped SQLite engine. Governed by `sqlite.md`.\n- PostgreSQL: selected only by a `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN through the same shipped library. Not verified by any acceptance check.\n- CLI: the `ledger` executable at the application root. Governed by `cli.md`.\n- pytest: test runner behind `bin/test.sh`. Governed by `python.md`.\n- POSIX sh: `bin/test.sh`, the `ledger` launcher, and the supplied grader. Governed by `common.md`.\n\n## Module Layout\n\n- `ledger`: POSIX launcher at the application root. It changes to the application root and runs `cli.main` under `.venv`.\n- `pyproject.toml` and `uv.lock`: dependency manifest and committed lock file.\n- `.venv/`: environment created by `uv venv`.\n- `bin/`: POSIX launchers, including `bin/test.sh`.\n- `config.py`: database target resolution from the environment.\n- `cli/`: `main(argv)` entry point, `cli_runner` test client, command dispatch, and CSV output.\n- `commands/`: one module per command group (accounts, categories, transactions, transfers, loads, reports). Each command body runs inside the command transaction wrapper.\n- `persistence/` and `schema.sql`: generated from `DATABASE.md` at the DATABASE step.\n- `tests/`: pytest suite for the application.\n\n## Boundaries\n\n- Call direction is `cli` to `commands` to `persistence`. The `cli` package never opens a database.\n- `commands` reach storage only through `db.<table>.<verb>(...)`, obtained from `persistence.open_database`.\n- Application code never imports `sqlite3` or any PostgreSQL driver. Only the shipped engines under `persistence/` do.\n- `config.py` reads the environment and returns the selected target as a string. It never opens a connection.\n- No command reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file except through `ledger load`.\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Low-level APIs it may access |\n|----------|-------|------------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` (generated and shipped) | Driver APIs inside `persistence/sqlite.py` and `persistence/postgres.py` only |\n| Configuration | `config.py` | `os.environ` for `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_PATH` |\n| File store | None | None. Seed files are read only by `ledger load`, through `commands/` |\n| External service | None | None |\n\n## Transaction and Balance Boundary\n\n- The `commands` layer opens one transaction per command and commits it only after every change succeeds.\n- Before commit, the balance rule is checked for every affected account. A negative balance rolls the command back and exits 3.\n- Concurrency relies on the database's own transaction isolation. No application-level locking is added.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC architecture-python-manifest ===\nIntent: pyproject.toml declares a project requiring Python 3.11 or newer.\n\nimport tomllib\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndata = tomllib.loads(Path(\"pyproject.toml\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\nrequires = data[\"project\"][\"requires-python\"]\nassert isinstance(requires, str)\nassert \"3.11\" in requires\n=== END AC architecture-python-manifest ===\n\n=== AC architecture-dependency-manifest ===\nIntent: pyproject.toml declares psycopg for the PostgreSQL engine and pytest for the test runner.\n\nimport tomllib\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndata = tomllib.loads(Path(\"pyproject.toml\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\ndependencies = list(data[\"project\"][\"dependencies\"])\ndev_group = list(data.get(\"dependency-groups\", {}).get(\"dev\", []))\nassert any(item.startswith(\"psycopg\") for item in dependencies)\nassert any(item.startswith(\"pytest\") for item in dependencies + dev_group)\n=== END AC architecture-dependency-manifest ===\n\n=== AC architecture-lock-and-venv ===\nIntent: uv.lock is committed at the application root and .venv exists as the environment directory.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nassert Path(\"uv.lock\").is_file()\nassert Path(\".venv\").is_dir()\n=== END AC architecture-lock-and-venv ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Application code never opens a database driver outside the shipped persistence library.\n- Money is stored and computed as integer cents at every layer.\n- No command writes outside the application root and its temporary test database.\n- No report row, count, or total is embedded in code.\n=== END ARTIFACT The value is\nreturned unchanged, including any `postgres://`, `postgresql://`, or `sqlite://` prefix, and is\npassed to `persistence.open_database` as the DSN.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- If `DATABASE_URL` is set and non-empty, its value is the target.\n- Otherwise, if `DATABASE_PATH` is set and non-empty, its value is the target.\n- Otherwise the target is `data/app.db`.\n- The function reads the environment only. It opens no connection.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC target-precedence ===\nIntent: DATABASE_URL takes precedence over DATABASE_PATH, which takes precedence over data/app.db, and each value is returned unchanged.\n\nimport os\nfrom config import resolve_database_target\n\nfor name in (\"DATABASE_URL\", \"DATABASE_PATH\"):\n    os.environ.pop(name, None)\ndefault_target = \"data/app.db\"\nassert resolve_database_target() == default_target\n\npath_value = \"scratch.db\"\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_PATH\"] = path_value\nassert resolve_database_target() == path_value\n\nurl_value = \"sqlite://\" + path_value\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = url_value\nassert resolve_database_target() == url_value\n\npostgres_value = \"postgresql://ledger@localhost/ledger\"\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = postgres_value\nassert resolve_database_target() == postgres_value\n=== END AC target-precedence ===\n\n=== AC target-opens-store ===\nIntent: The resolved target opens through the shipped persistence library and yields an empty accounts table.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom config import resolve_database_target\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nos.environ.pop(\"DATABASE_URL\", None)\nscratch_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath_value = str(Path(scratch_dir) / \"ledger.db\")\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_PATH\"] = path_value\ntarget = resolve_database_target()\nassert target == path_value\nwith db_session(target) as db:\n    rows = db.accounts.list()\n    print(rows)\n    assert rows == []\n=== END AC target-opens-store ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The target is never opened by this module; only persistence opens connections.\n- No default target other than `data/app.db` is applied when either setting is present.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliEntry.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Entry\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Provides the ledger executable at the application root and the cli.main entry point with its test client. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | ledger, cli.main, cli.testing.cli_runner |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger` is the only command-line entry point. It is a POSIX launcher at the application root.\nIt changes into the application root and runs `cli.main(argv)` under the `.venv` interpreter,\nthen exits with the returned status. `cli.main` takes an argument list and returns an integer\nstatus. `cli.testing.cli_runner(*args)` calls `main` in-process and returns `(exit_code, stdout,\nstderr)`.\n\n## Launcher Contract\n\n```\n#!/bin/sh\nHERE=$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")\" && pwd)\ncd \"$HERE\" && exec \"$HERE/.venv/bin/python\" -c 'import sys; from cli import main; sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))' \"$@\"\n```\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-entry-executable ===\nIntent: The ledger executable is a file at the application root with its execute bit set.\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nledger_path = Path(\"ledger\")\nassert ledger_path.is_file()\nassert os.access(ledger_path, os.X_OK)\n=== END AC cli-entry-executable ===\n\n=== AC cli-entry-main-contract ===\nIntent: cli_runner returns an integer status and two strings, and the ledger executable exits with the same status for the same argv.\n\nimport subprocess\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nargs = [\"no-such-command\"]\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\nassert isinstance(code, int)\nassert isinstance(out, str)\nassert isinstance(err, str)\nproc = subprocess.run([str(Path(\"ledger\").resolve()), *args], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(proc.stdout)\nprint(proc.stderr)\nassert proc.returncode == code\n=== END AC cli-entry-main-contract ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The `ledger` launcher never invokes a system Python for command execution; it always uses `.venv`.\n- The cli package never opens a database connection.\n- The launcher writes nothing to standard output of its own.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliDispatch.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Dispatch\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Routes command words to handlers, rejects usage errors with exit 2, and provides help for every command. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CliEntry.md |\n| Provides    | command dispatch, ledger --help, ledger <command> --help |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`cli.main` reads the first argument as the command word and dispatches it. Unknown commands,\nunknown tables, unknown reports, and wrong argument counts are usage errors: they exit 2, write\nnothing to standard output, and write one diagnostic line to standard error. Every command has\nhelp text. `--help` and `<command> --help` write usage to standard output and exit 0. With no\narguments, `main` writes one usage line to standard error and exits 2.\n\n## Commands Recognized\n\n`init`, `count`, `load`, `account` (`add`, `get`, `rename`, `delete`), `txn` (`post`, `get`,\n`amend`, `delete`), `transfer`, `report` (`balances`, `categories`, `monthly`).\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC dispatch-help ===\nIntent: ledger --help and the --help of each recognized command print usage to standard output and exit 0.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nhelp_cases = [\n    [\"--help\"],\n    [\"init\", \"--help\"],\n    [\"count\", \"--help\"],\n BEGIN ARTIFACT ARCHITECTURE.md ===\n# ARCHITECTURE: Cash Ledger\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Module layout, boundaries, and technology decisions for the command-line cash ledger. |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe `ledger` executable is a command-line cash ledger for a small shop. Commands read CSV on\nstandard output, diagnostics on standard error, and exit codes 0 to 4. Every command runs as one\ndatabase transaction. All persistence goes through the shipped persistence library, and the\ndatabase is selected by `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- Python 3.11 or newer: implementation language. Governed by `python.md`.\n- SQLite: database engine, reached through `persistence.open_database` and the shipped SQLite engine. Governed by `sqlite.md`.\n- PostgreSQL: selected only by a `postgres://` or `postgresql://` DSN through the same shipped library. Not verified by any acceptance check.\n- CLI: the `ledger` executable at the application root. Governed by `cli.md`.\n- pytest: test runner behind `bin/test.sh`. Governed by `python.md`.\n- POSIX sh: `bin/test.sh`, the `ledger` launcher, and the supplied grader. Governed by `common.md`.\n\n## Module Layout\n\n- `ledger`: POSIX launcher at the application root. It changes to the application root and runs `cli.main` under `.venv`.\n- `pyproject.toml` and `uv.lock`: dependency manifest and committed lock file.\n- `.venv/`: environment created by `uv venv`.\n- `bin/`: POSIX launchers, including `bin/test.sh`.\n- `config.py`: database target resolution from the environment.\n- `cli/`: `main(argv)` entry point, `cli_runner` test client, command dispatch, and CSV output.\n- `commands/`: one module per command group (accounts, categories, transactions, transfers, loads, reports). Each command body runs inside the command transaction wrapper.\n- `persistence/` and `schema.sql`: generated from `DATABASE.md` at the DATABASE step.\n- `tests/`: pytest suite for the application.\n\n## Boundaries\n\n- Call direction is `cli` to `commands` to `persistence`. The `cli` package never opens a database.\n- `commands` reach storage only through `db.<table>.<verb>(...)`, obtained from `persistence.open_database`.\n- Application code never imports `sqlite3` or any PostgreSQL driver. Only the shipped engines under `persistence/` do.\n- `config.py` reads the environment and returns the selected target as a string. It never opens a connection.\n- No command reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file except through `ledger load`.\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Low-level APIs it may access |\n|----------|-------|------------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` (generated and shipped) | Driver APIs inside `persistence/sqlite.py` and `persistence/postgres.py` only |\n| Configuration | `config.py` | `os.environ` for `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_PATH` |\n| File store | None | None. Seed files are read only by `ledger load`, through `commands/` |\n| External service | None | None |\n\n## Transaction and Balance Boundary\n\n- The `commands` layer opens one transaction per command and commits it only after every change succeeds.\n- Before commit, the balance rule is checked for every affected account. A negative balance rolls the command back and exits 3.\n- Concurrency relies on the database's own transaction isolation. No application-level locking is added.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC architecture-python-manifest ===\nIntent: pyproject.toml declares a project requiring Python 3.11 or newer.\n\nimport tomllib\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndata = tomllib.loads(Path(\"pyproject.toml\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\nrequires = data[\"project\"][\"requires-python\"]\nassert isinstance(requires, str)\nassert \"3.11\" in requires\n=== END AC architecture-python-manifest ===\n\n=== AC architecture-dependency-manifest ===\nIntent: pyproject.toml declares psycopg for the PostgreSQL engine and pytest for the test runner.\n\nimport tomllib\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndata = tomllib.loads(Path(\"pyproject.toml\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\ndependencies = list(data[\"project\"][\"dependencies\"])\ndev_group = list(data.get(\"dependency-groups\", {}).get(\"dev\", []))\nassert any(item.startswith(\"psycopg\") for item in dependencies)\nassert any(item.startswith(\"pytest\") for item in dependencies + dev_group)\n=== END AC architecture-dependency-manifest ===\n\n=== AC architecture-lock-and-venv ===\nIntent: uv.lock is committed at the application root and .venv exists as the environment directory.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nassert Path(\"uv.lock\").is_file()\nassert Path(\".venv\").is_dir()\n=== END AC architecture-lock-and-venv ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Application code never opens a database driver outside the shipped persistence library.\n- Money is stored and computed as integer cents at every layer.\n- No command writes outside the application root and its temporary test database.\n- No report row, count, or total is embedded in code.\n=== END ARTIFACT The value is\nreturned unchanged, including any `postgres://`, `postgresql://`, or `sqlite://` prefix, and is\npassed to `persistence.open_database` as the DSN.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- If `DATABASE_URL` is set and non-empty, its value is the target.\n- Otherwise, if `DATABASE_PATH` is set and non-empty, its value is the target.\n- Otherwise the target is `data/app.db`.\n- The function reads the environment only. It opens no connection.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC target-precedence ===\nIntent: DATABASE_URL takes precedence over DATABASE_PATH, which takes precedence over data/app.db, and each value is returned unchanged.\n\nimport os\nfrom config import resolve_database_target\n\nfor name in (\"DATABASE_URL\", \"DATABASE_PATH\"):\n    os.environ.pop(name, None)\ndefault_target = \"data/app.db\"\nassert resolve_database_target() == default_target\n\npath_value = \"scratch.db\"\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_PATH\"] = path_value\nassert resolve_database_target() == path_value\n\nurl_value = \"sqlite://\" + path_value\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = url_value\nassert resolve_database_target() == url_value\n\npostgres_value = \"postgresql://ledger@localhost/ledger\"\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = postgres_value\nassert resolve_database_target() == postgres_value\n=== END AC target-precedence ===\n\n=== AC target-opens-store ===\nIntent: The resolved target opens through the shipped persistence library and yields an empty accounts table.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom config import resolve_database_target\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nos.environ.pop(\"DATABASE_URL\", None)\nscratch_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath_value = str(Path(scratch_dir) / \"ledger.db\")\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_PATH\"] = path_value\ntarget = resolve_database_target()\nassert target == path_value\nwith db_session(target) as db:\n    rows = db.accounts.list()\n    print(rows)\n    assert rows == []\n=== END AC target-opens-store ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The target is never opened by this module; only persistence opens connections.\n- No default target other than `data/app.db` is applied when either setting is present.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliEntry.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Entry\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Provides the ledger executable at the application root and the cli.main entry point with its test client. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | ledger, cli.main, cli.testing.cli_runner |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger` is the only command-line entry point. It is a POSIX launcher at the application root.\nIt changes into the application root and runs `cli.main(argv)` under the `.venv` interpreter,\nthen exits with the returned status. `cli.main` takes an argument list and returns an integer\nstatus. `cli.testing.cli_runner(*args)` calls `main` in-process and returns `(exit_code, stdout,\nstderr)`.\n\n## Launcher Contract\n\n```\n#!/bin/sh\nHERE=$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")\" && pwd)\ncd \"$HERE\" && exec \"$HERE/.venv/bin/python\" -c 'import sys; from cli import main; sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))' \"$@\"\n```\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-entry-executable ===\nIntent: The ledger executable is a file at the application root with its execute bit set.\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nledger_path = Path(\"ledger\")\nassert ledger_path.is_file()\nassert os.access(ledger_path, os.X_OK)\n=== END AC cli-entry-executable ===\n\n=== AC cli-entry-main-contract ===\nIntent: cli_runner returns an integer status and two strings, and the ledger executable exits with the same status for the same argv.\n\nimport subprocess\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nargs = [\"no-such-command\"]\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\nassert isinstance(code, int)\nassert isinstance(out, str)\nassert isinstance(err, str)\nproc = subprocess.run([str(Path(\"ledger\").resolve()), *args], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(proc.stdout)\nprint(proc.stderr)\nassert proc.returncode == code\n=== END AC cli-entry-main-contract ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The `ledger` launcher never invokes a system Python for command execution; it always uses `.venv`.\n- The cli package never opens a database connection.\n- The launcher writes nothing to standard output of its own.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliDispatch.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Dispatch\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Routes command words to handlers, rejects usage errors with exit 2, and provides help for every command. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CliEntry.md |\n| Provides    | command dispatch, ledger --help, ledger <command> --help |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`cli.main` reads the first argument as the command word and dispatches it. Unknown commands,\nunknown tables, unknown reports, and wrong argument counts are usage errors: they exit 2, write\nnothing to standard output, and write one diagnostic line to standard error. Every command has\nhelp text. `--help` and `<command> --help` write usage to standard output and exit 0. With no\narguments, `main` writes one usage line to standard error and exits 2.\n\n## Commands Recognized\n\n`init`, `count`, `load`, `account` (`add`, `get`, `rename`, `delete`), `txn` (`post`, `get`,\n`amend`, `delete`), `transfer`, `report` (`balances`, `categories`, `monthly`).\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC dispatch-help ===\nIntent: ledger --help and the --help of each recognized command print usage to standard output and exit 0.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nhelp_cases = [\n    [\"--help\"],\n    [\"init\", \"--help\"],\n    [\"count\", \"--help\"],\n    [\"load\", \"--help\"],\n    [\"account\", \"add\", \"--help\"],\n    [\"txn\", \"post\", \"--help\"],\n    [\"transfer\", \"--help\"],\n    [\"report\", \"balances\", \"--help\"],\n]\nfor argv in help_cases:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(*argv)\n    print(argv, code, err)\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out.strip() != \"\"\n=== END AC dispatch-help ===\n\n=== AC dispatch-unknown-command ===\nIntent: An unknown command exits 2 and writes one diagnostic line to standard error and nothing to standard output.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nunknown = \"frobnicate\"\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(unknown)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 2\nassert out == \"\"\nassert len([line for line in err.splitlines() if line.strip()]) == 1\n=== END AC dispatch-unknown-command ===\n\n=== AC dispatch-usage-errors ===\nIntent: An unknown table, an unknown report, and wrong argument counts each exit 2 with nothing on standard output.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nusage_cases = [\n    [\"load\", \"nosuch\", \"bogus.csv\"],\n    [\"report\", \"nosuch\"],\n    [\"txn\", \"get\"],\n    [\"init\", \"extra\"],\n]\nfor argv in usage_cases:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(*argv)\n    print(argv, code, err)\n    assert code == 2\n    assert out == \"\"\n=== END AC dispatch-usage-errors ===\n\n=== AC dispatch-no-arguments ===\nIntent: Invoking ledger with no arguments writes one usage line to standard error and exits 2.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner()\nprint(err)\nassert code == 2\nassert out == \"\"\nassert len([line for line in err.splitlines() if line.strip()]) == 1\n=== END AC dispatch-no-arguments ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A usage error never opens the database.\n- Help text is written only to standard output, never to standard error.\n- Standard error is diagnostic only and is never the success path.\n=== END ARTIFACT
